Our work demonstrates that reconstruction-based representation learning is a promising direction for 3D medical imaging under limited data availability.

</details>


### [35] [Rethinking Cross-Sensor Domain Adaptation for Bridge Point Cloud Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27060)
*Linghui Huang,Zipeng Wang,Shunjie Chen,Peng Guan,Jiandong Wang,Siqing Li,Luming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究重新审视桥梁点云TLS→MLS语义分割的域适应协议，发现8cm稀疏体素表示下采样密度、表面粗糙度、颜色和视角差异的影响有限，而适应增益高度依赖源模型收敛程度与评估波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁点云分割模型在TLS数据训练后迁移到MLS数据时精度下降，需厘清TLS与MLS差异在稀疏体素表示中的具体表现形式，以及域适应增益为何不稳定。

Method: 采用单个稀疏卷积U-Net，结合原始点统计与可控输入扰动分析采样密度、粗糙度、颜色和视角的影响；在100与300 epoch两种训练计划下比较自适应批归一化、熵最小化、Mean Teacher和置信度过滤自训练。

Result: 预处理后TLS与MLS最近邻间距相近（12.08 vs 12.02 mm），4 mm扰动仅改变体素占据率4.4%；颜色差异系统但消色仅带来+0.02 mIoU（尺度校正后+0.06）；300 epoch源模型在配对桥梁协议上达57.80 mIoU；置信度过滤自训练对收敛模型反而使目标mIoU下降0.97（源选检查点）和2.44（末50 epoch均值）；重复评估标准差0.27–0.64，种子间差异达1.88，与方法间差异相当。

Conclusion: 该基准上的域适应结论受表示尺度、源模型收敛程度和评估变异性共同制约，方法间比较需谨慎对待。

Abstract: Segmentation models for bridge point clouds lose accuracy when trained on terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) data and applied to mobile laser scanning (MLS) data. This study re-examines the SemanticBridge TLS-to-MLS protocol to determine how sampling density, surface roughness, color, and acquisition viewpoint are expressed in the 8-cm sparse-voxel representation, and how adaptation gains depend on source-model convergence. Using one sparse-convolution U-Net, we combine raw-point statistics with controlled input perturbations and compare adaptive batch normalization, entropy minimization, Mean Teacher, and confidence-filtered self-training under 100- and 300-epoch schedules. TLS and MLS have similar nearest-neighbor spacing after preprocessing (12.08 and 12.02 mm), and a 4-mm perturbation of TLS points changes voxel occupancy by only 4.4%. Color distributions differ systematically, but zeroing color on identical voxels changes target mean intersection over union (mIoU) by only +0.02, and by +0.06 after the color augmentation is scale-corrected. The sensors also observe different portions of the bridge, yielding different class proportions. A 300-epoch source-only model obtains 57.80 mIoU on the paired-bridge protocol. Confidence-filtered self-training improves the 100-epoch baseline, but from a converged model it changes target mIoU by -0.97 (source-selected checkpoints) and -2.44 (final-50-epoch means). Repeated evaluation (standard deviation 0.27-0.64) and seed variation (1.88 across three seeds) are comparable to several method differences. Adaptation results on this benchmark therefore depend on representation scale, source-model convergence, and evaluation variability.

</details>


### [36] [V2VFusion: Text-Controlled Video-to-Video Diffusion for Degradation-Aware Video Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27063)
*Jiajun Chen,Han Xu,Yunfei Huang,Zhuoya Zhang,Guangcan Liu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: V2VFusion是首个统一的video-to-video融合框架，将视频融合重构为潜空间扩散中的视频到视频条件生成问题，从退化多源输入直接生成时序连贯的高质量融合视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法逐帧融合，缺乏跨序列的统一时空建模，破坏时序一致性；面对退化时忽略时序动态，并用单一网络承载多种退化，导致退化抑制有限且不均衡。

Method: 1) 在潜扩散空间中将视频融合重构为video-to-video条件生成，整体建模全序列时空依赖以保证序列级一致性；2) 文本引导的退化条件ControlNet将高层文本退化描述与低层视觉先验桥接，实现可解释、可控的结构保持融合；3) 状态调制的退化感知分层混合专家（MoE）将专家选择解耦为扩散状态感知过滤与退化感知路由，避免不可靠路由并促进专家专门化。

Result: 在红外-可见光、多曝光、多聚焦视频融合任务及复杂退化下，融合质量与时序稳定性均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 统一视频到视频扩散框架可有效解决帧级融合的时序不一致与退化抑制不均衡问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video fusion integrates complementary information in multiple source video streams into a single sequence for complete scene perception. Existing methods perform frame-wise fusion and lack unified spatio-temporal modeling across sequences, damaging temporal coherence across sequences. When dealing with degradations, they also ignore temporal dynamics and overload a single network with diverse degradations, leading to limited and imbalanced degradation suppression. To address these issues, we propose V2VFusion, the first unified video-to-video fusion framework that directly generates temporally coherent, high-quality fused videos from degraded multi-source inputs. By reformulating video fusion as a video-to-video conditional generation problem in latent diffusion space, our approach holistically models spatio-temporal dependencies across entire sequences and intrinsically ensures sequence-level consistency. On this basis, a text-guided degradation-conditioned ControlNet bridges high-level textual degradation descriptions with low-level visual priors, enabling interpretable and controllable guidance for structure-preserving fusion beyond purely data-driven conditioning. To handle heterogeneous and compound degradations, a state-modulated degradation-aware hierarchical mixture-of-experts module decouples expert selection into diffusion-state-aware filtering and degradation-aware routing. It prevents unreliable routing and fosters expert specialization to mitigate representation conflicts among diverse degradations. Experiments on infrared-visible, multi-exposure, and multi-focus video fusion tasks and complex degradations validate that V2VFusion surpasses existing methods in fusion quality and temporal stability. The code is available at https://github.com/ChenDoubleJ/V2VFusion.

</details>


### [37] [$R^2$: Recognizing and Reducing Redundant Visual Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27070)
*Yilin Zhao,Jinyu Wang,Wenming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 R² 框架，识别并减少视觉语言模型中冗余的视觉工具调用；用 CAT 分析样本级工具效用、VFD 度量单次调用依赖，并以 RA-GRPO 在相当精度下减少 12.39% 工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示视觉工具能提升多模态推理，但工具效用样本间差异大，基准准确率无法区分工具交互是有益还是冗余，缺乏对单次调用价值的评估手段。

Method: 1) 样本级 CAT（Category Analysis of Tool Utility）按样本能否从工具获益进行分类；2) 提出 VFD（Visual Feedback Dependency）指标，在真实视觉反馈与替代视觉反馈下比较同一后续推理的似然，无需外部评估器或轨迹重生成即可评估单次调用；3) RA-GRPO，将 VFD 依赖信号与类别条件下的基础调用成本结合，在正确轨迹间分配相对效率优势，补充结果监督。

Result: 七个基准上，获益样本最多仅占单个基准的 12.44%，多数样本获益甚微甚至受损，基准分数掩盖了工具使用能力；RA-GRPO 相比 Vanilla GRPO 平均减少 12.39% 工具调用且精度相当，按工具效用类别宏平均精度提升 3.35 个百分点。

Conclusion: 基准准确率不足以反映工具使用能力，需样本级与调用级评估；R² 通过效用分类与依赖度量化实现冗余工具调用的识别与抑制，在保持精度的同时提升工具使用效率与类别均衡性能。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that visual tools can improve multimodal reasoning. However, the effects of tools vary substantially across samples, and benchmark accuracy alone does not reveal whether tool interactions are beneficial or redundant. We present $R^2$, a framework that recognizes and reduces redundant visual tool use in vision-language models. We first introduce a sample-level Category Analysis of Tool Utility (CAT), which categorizes samples according to whether a model can benefit from tools. This analysis reveals that benchmark scores may obscure a model's tool-use ability. Across seven benchmarks, samples benefiting from tools account for at most 12.44% of each benchmark, while the majority gain little from tool interaction or are even harmed by it. To assess individual calls, we propose the Visual Feedback Dependency (VFD) metric, which compares the likelihood of the same subsequent reasoning under actual and alternative visual feedback without an external evaluator or trajectory regeneration. Redundancy-Aware GRPO (RA-GRPO) combines this dependency signal with a category-conditioned base call cost to assign relative efficiency advantages among correct trajectories, supplementing outcome-based supervision. Across the seven benchmarks, RA-GRPO reduces the mean number of tool calls by 12.39% relative to Vanilla GRPO at comparable benchmark accuracy. Evaluated by tool-utility category, RA-GRPO improves category macro-average accuracy over Vanilla GRPO by 3.35 percentage points.

</details>


### [38] [Hindsight Forcing: Learning Better History from the Future in Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27074)
*Haonan An,Yihang Tao,Yu Guo,Renlong Chen,Jiasheng Zhang,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Hindsight Forcing，通过潜空间直通桥（latent straight-through bridge）解耦前向生成与反向信用分配，使历史 chunk 能直接从未来损失获得梯度，无需额外参数或架构改动即可缓解少步自回归视频扩散中的视觉漂移与结构崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频扩散可实现实时流式生成，每个生成 chunk 作为后续生成的历史上下文。但为避免 BPTT 的显存爆炸，现有 self-rollout 方法通常截断跨 chunk 梯度或 detach 历史 latent，导致早期去噪计算无法通过生成的历史获得下游反馈（即不知道如何合成有利于后续生成的帧），随 rollout 延长出现视觉漂移与结构崩溃。

Method: Hindsight Forcing：前向传播中每个 chunk 完成完整多步 rollout 以更新 KV cache；反向传播中通过 latent 直通桥将未来损失梯度回传至每个历史 chunk 中单一选定的去噪步，使 context writer 与历史 denoiser 共享参数联合训练，而无需保留完整轨迹计算图。无额外参数、无架构修改、无独立轨迹重放。

Result: 在 VBench 上的大量实验表明，Hindsight Forcing 在整体视觉质量与动态运动方面优于现有基线；消融研究验证了对历史去噪进行监督的收益。

Conclusion: 通过直通桥实现历史 chunk 与未来结果之间的信用分配，可在低显存开销下为历史去噪提供有效监督，为长程自回归视频生成的高保真流式生成提供了一种轻量方案。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video diffusion enables real-time streaming generation, with each generated chunk serving as historical context for subsequent generation. To avoid the memory explosion of backpropagation through time, previous self-rollout methods typically truncate cross-chunk gradients or detach historical latents. As a result, earlier denoising computations receive no downstream feedback through the generated history on how to synthesize frames that facilitate subsequent generation, leading to visual drift and structural collapse as rollouts extend. To address this limitation, we propose Hindsight Forcing, which allows historical chunks to learn directly from future outcomes by decoupling forward generation from backward credit assignment via a latent straight-through bridge. In the forward pass, each chunk completes a full multi-step rollout to update the KV cache. In the backward pass, the bridge routes gradients from future losses back to a single selected denoising step per historical chunk, training both the context writer and historical denoiser on shared parameters without retaining the full trajectory graph. Remarkably, Hindsight Forcing requires no additional model parameters, architecture modifications, or separate trajectory replay. Extensive experiments on VBench demonstrate that Hindsight Forcing outperforms existing baselines in overall visual quality and dynamic motion, while ablation studies validate the benefit of supervising historical denoising. Code is available at https://github.com/haonanAN309/Hindsight-Forcing.

</details>


### [39] [PIU-CR: Physics-Informed Deep Unfolding with SAR-Optical Image Fusion for Cloud Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27093)
*Gengque Fan,Han Xu,Junxi Li,Hao Zhang,Guangcan Liu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理信息深度展开网络PIU-CR，用于光学-SAR融合去云，将大气散射模型嵌入数据保真项、以SAR引导的结构与光谱正则建立显式跨模态交互，并展开为可解释多阶段网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去云方法多为黑盒数据驱动，跨模态交互可解释性差；已有物理先验引入方式过于简化，无法真实刻画成像物理过程，且网络与优化变量耦合松散。

Method: 将去云建模为物理信息优化问题：数据保真项嵌入大气散射模型以刻画云退化成像机制；正则项构建显式跨模态交互（SAR引导结构正则与光谱正则，含跨模态结构特征对齐与协调光谱一致性约束）；再将优化问题展开为含结构与光谱双分支的多阶段神经网络，每阶段对应一次显式优化迭代。

Result: 实验表明该方法优于现有最先进方法，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将物理成像模型与优化展开结合，PIU-CR在提升去云性能的同时增强了跨模态交互与重建过程的可解释性。

Abstract: Multi-modal cloud removal exploits complementary optical and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images to restore cloud-degraded remote sensing images. Most existing methods rely on black-box, data-driven architectures with limited interpretability of cross-modal interactions. Although some attempts introduce physical priors into networks, their oversimplified formulations fail to faithfully characterize the underlying physical imaging process and the networks remain loosely coupled with optimization variables. To address these issues, we propose a physics-informed deep unfolding network for cloud removal via optical-SAR image fusion, termed as PIU-CR. We first formulate cloud removal as a physics-informed optimization problem. Specifically, we fundamentally ground the optimization in the physical imaging mechanism of cloud degradation by embedding the atmospheric scattering model into the data fidelity term. Then, the regularization terms establish an explicit cross-modal interaction mechanism, overcoming the single-modal information scarcity and the obscure, uninterpretable interactions in black-box models. They are dedicated to SAR-guided structure and spectral regularization, jointly introducing cross-modal structural feature alignment and coordinated spectral consistency constraints. The resulting optimization problem is then unfolded into a multi-stage neural network with dedicated structure and spectral branches. Each stage corresponds to an explicit optimization iteration step, enabling interpretable and effective reconstruction process. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Fgq188/PIU_CR.

</details>


### [40] [UDA-Restoration: Bridging Unsupervised Domain Adaptation and Multi-Degradation Denoising for Enhanced AV Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27097)
*Zainab Bibi,Mohsin Kamal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出扩散引导的无监督域适应图像恢复算法UDA-Restoration（基于Noise-Space Domain Adaptation范式），可增强与去噪自动驾驶图像，对未见退化场景鲁棒，性能优于自监督/无监督基线并接近最佳监督恢复方法，同时提升下游语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶视觉感知系统受恶劣天气、传感器噪声、运动伪影和光照变化影响导致图像质量下降，进而削弱感知性能；需在缺乏目标域标注的情况下实现稳健的图像恢复。

Method: 提出UDA-Restoration，遵循Noise-Space Domain Adaptation（Noise-DA）范式：多源退化学习 + 扩散引导的域适应，跨越合成与真实退化分布进行适配，实现图像增强与去噪。

Result: 在雪、雾、传感器畸变等未见退化场景下验证稳健性；定量结果显示Noise-DA优于所评估的自监督与无监督基线，并达到最佳监督恢复方法的水平；语义分割实验表明下游感知任务性能随图像恢复质量提升而直接改善。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的无监督域适应是自动驾驶图像恢复任务中强大且可扩展的方法，适用于多种环境。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles rely heavily on vision-based perception systems for safe navigation and decision-making. Adverse weather conditions, sensor noise, moving artifacts and illumination changes, however, often degrade the image quality and hence reduce perception performance. This paper introduces a diffusion-guided unsupervised domain adaptation algorithm for autonomous driving environments, namely UDA-Restoration, which is designed to enhance and denoise images. The proposed method follows the paradigm of Noise-Space Domain Adaptation (Noise-DA), which is based on multi-source degradation learning and diffusion-guided adaptation that spans across synthetic and real-world degradations. The framework has been extensively tested and shown to restore image quality and to be robust to unseen degradation scenarios, such as snow, haze, sensor distortions, and more. The quantitative results indicate that Noise-DA outperforms the evaluated self-supervised and unsupervised baselines while matching the best supervised restoration performance. In addition, semantic segmentation experiments show that the performance of downstream perception tasks is directly improved by better image restoration. The outcomes show the promise of unsupervised domain adaptation based on diffusion as a powerful and scalable method for image restoration tasks in autonomous driving systems under various environments.

</details>


### [41] [EgoSurgHands: An Egocentric 3D Hand Pose Dataset & Benchmark for Open Surgery Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27101)
*Ankit Pal,Guillaume Kugener,Sofia Volpi,Joshua Schwartz,Alex Idarraga,Pranav Rajpurkar,Gabriel A. Brat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoSurgHands——首个真实采集的外科开放手术训练场景自我中心双手关节3D姿态数据集与基准，含多传感器数据与严格人工校验标注，并系统评测八种零样本方法与一个轻量残差校正基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术技能评估、AR引导与机器人示教学习均依赖对术者双手的3D跟踪，但现有手术数据集缺乏真实采集的自我中心关节式3D手部姿态数据。

Method: 用多传感器眼镜在合成垫上的开放手术训练中采集数据：41,909条训练手部实例（55段录制）、9,476条人工校验评测（13段留出录制），覆盖13项任务、3种手套颜色、4名受训者；每帧含内参、带置信度的手部关键点、时间戳、眼动注视，部分含SLAM外参。标签由传感器融合伪真值、逐帧人工复核、外科医生裁定与独立多视角三角化参考构成。设置过程不相交划分与留一佩戴者协议，零样本评测MANO重建法、IntagHand、MediaPipe Hands等八种方法，并提出58K参数残差校正器JRN。

Result: 自我中心预训练在各留出佩戴者上迁移更优：WildHands与JointTransformer的PA-MPJPE分别比非自我中心的HaMeR低18%与40%；JointTransformer最优，误差14.95 mm；MediaPipe Hands仅在24.1%的评测行检出双手；JRN在冻结骨干上于留出佩戴者达10.96–14.85 mm。

Conclusion: 手术域差距仍显著；自我中心预训练骨干结合轻量校正可有效缩小误差；数据集与基准代码已公开。

Abstract: Surgical skill assessment, AR guidance, and robot learning from demonstration all benefit from 3D tracking of the surgeon's hands, yet no surgical dataset offers real egocentric articulated 3D hand pose. We introduce EgoSurgHands, a benchmark for real-captured egocentric hand pose estimation in open surgery training on synthetic pads. Captured with multi-sensor glasses, it contains 41,909 training hand-instance rows across 55 procedure recordings and 9,476 human-validated evaluation rows across 13 held-out recordings, spanning 13 tasks, 3 glove colors, and 4 trainees from first-year medical student to third-year resident. Each frame carries a multi-sensor stack: intrinsics, hand landmarks with per-hand confidence, timestamps, and eye gaze, plus SLAM extrinsics where SLAM succeeded. Evaluation labels combine sensor-fused pseudo-ground truth, human cross-review of every frame, surgeon adjudication of flagged cases, and an independent multi-view triangulated reference. Alongside the within-corpus, procedure-disjoint split, a leave-one-wearer-out protocol tested unseen trainees. Across eight methods evaluated zero-shot, from MANO-based reconstructors to the two-hand IntagHand and the keypoint-only MediaPipe Hands, egocentric pretraining was associated with stronger transfer on every held-out wearer. WildHands and JointTransformer had 18\% and 40\% lower PA-MPJPE, respectively, than the non-egocentric HaMeR. JointTransformer was the best performing model with an error of 14.95 mm. A substantial surgical domain gap remains, and the widely deployed MediaPipe Hands detected a hand in only 24.1\% of evaluation rows. As a baseline, a 58K-parameter residual corrector (JRN) on frozen backbones reached 10.96-14.85 mm PA-MPJPE on held-out wearers for the two ego-pretrained backbones. We release the dataset and benchmark code at https://egosurghands.github.io

</details>


### [42] [One state, many questions: Opsis gives a frontier model typed eyes for pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27102)
*Zhixuan Ge,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Opsis——一个在冻结病理编码器上的"类型化提问接口"，让语言模型以算子+参数+候选的方式向切片发问而非阅读描述或看缩略图，从而在乳腺与泛癌病理报告问答上显著优于前沿模型的"读描述/看切片"方式，并可组合回答未见过的复合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化病理报告是具固定答案集的清单式问题，且许多问题由其他问题组合而成。现有两条路线各有缺陷：病理基础模型需为每个问题单独训练分类器，扩展性差；前沿语言模型能自由组合，但无法把一张含约 1.4 万个 patch 的切片放进上下文，其"读文字描述"（描述在问题已知前写就、与问题无关）或"看缩略图+少量 patch"（覆盖有限）的作答准确率仅 0.50–0.66，低于多数类基线 0.67。

Method: 在冻结的 Virchow2 编码器之上构建"类型化问题接口"：切片仅编码一次；问题在运行时指定算子、参数与候选集，模型输出候选上的概率分布。实现两个版本——Opsis-S（冻结编码器上的 10M 参数解码器）与 Opsis-V（嵌入 Qwen3-VL-8B）。评测：189 道乳腺报告题、3,797 道泛癌题、995 道未训练过的复合题（由前沿模型组合 Opsis-S 的答复）、10,712 张 TCGA 切片的泛癌题集，以及 CAMELYON16 迁移与坏死定位探测。

Result: 四个前沿模型通过向 Opsis-S 提问，乳腺题准确率达 0.78–0.79；泛癌题上"提问"比"阅读"高 8.6 个百分点。未训练的 995 道复合题上，组合 Opsis-S 答复达 0.76，高于看切片的 0.69，接近逐复合题训练 ABMIL 的 0.78。TCGA 泛癌题集上单个 Opsis-S 解码器与逐题训练的 TITAN/PRISM2 线性探针相当（0.69 vs 0.70/0.68）。此外，未在该队列训练即区分 CAMELYON16 转移灶（AUROC 0.86），并仅凭报告标签定位坏死（tile AUROC 0.93）。

Conclusion: 将"阅读/观看"转为"提问"是使通用语言模型在结构化病理报告上获得可靠、可组合答题能力的关键：单一轻量解码器可替代大量逐题分类器，并具备零样本迁移与弱监督定位能力。

Abstract: A synoptic pathology report is a checklist of questions with fixed answer sets, many composed from others. Pathology foundation models answer them accurately with one classifier trained with labels for each, and frontier language models, which compose freely, cannot fit a slide of fourteen thousand tiles in their context. On 189 breast report questions, frontier models that read a written description of the slide or look at a thumbnail and tiles of it answer at 0.50 to 0.66, below the majority class at 0.67. A description is written before the question is known, and a look covers a handful of tiles. We present Opsis, which lets the model ask instead: a typed question interface on a frozen pathology encoder. A slide is encoded once, a question names an operator, arguments and candidates given at runtime, and the answer is a probability over the candidates. We build it as a 10 M parameter decoder on frozen Virchow2 tokens (Opsis-S) and inside Qwen3-VL-8B (Opsis-V). Asking Opsis-S, four frontier models answer the breast questions at 0.78 to 0.79, and on 3,797 pan cancer questions asking is 8.6 points above reading. On 995 composite questions that Opsis-S was never trained on, a frontier model composing its answers reaches 0.76, against 0.69 when it looks at the slide and 0.78 for ABMIL trained on each composite. On a pan cancer question set over 10,712 TCGA slides, one Opsis-S decoder matches linear probes trained per question on TITAN and PRISM2 embeddings (average accuracy 0.69 against 0.70 and 0.68). Opsis-S also separates CAMELYON16 metastases without training on that cohort (AUROC 0.86) and localises necrosis from report labels alone (tile AUROC 0.93).

</details>


### [43] [RemoteEvolver: Expanding Earth Observation Capabilities through Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27110)
*Shengxiang Xu,Liang Yao,Shuyi Chen,Yishu Wang,Shimin Di,Fan Liu,Bishun Yao,Hanghui Guo,Xinyu Liu,Chengye Liang,Yuyu Luo,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RemoteEvolver：一个外部harness，通过执行反馈驱动遥感智能体的工具与工作流演化，在基础模型参数固定的前提下按需获取缺失工具并把可复用经验蒸馏为技能，在Earth-Bench与OpenEarthAgent-Bench上分别平均提升8.18与23.86个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是构建可持续自我改进的遥感智能体系统，使其能识别并弥补自身能力缺口并保留改进。现有系统依赖固定工具集，经验积累仅局限于重新编排预定义能力，导致能力扩展受限、改进难以跨任务迁移。

Method: 将harness视为基础模型之外管理工具与工作流知识的可编辑持久资源，从而无需后训练即可更新与复用。RemoteEvolver在固定基础模型参数下，依据执行反馈进行工具与工作流演化：按需获取缺失的专用工具，并将单个任务中经验证的成功执行经验蒸馏为可跨任务复用的agent skills；所获工具与累积技能共同支撑后续任务的规划与执行。

Result: 在Earth-Bench与OpenEarthAgent-Bench上，相较初始状态，工具完全可用与部分缺失两种设置下平均精度分别提升8.18与23.86个百分点；相同设置下相较SOTA方法平均分别领先6.77与17.10个百分点。

Conclusion: 外部harness式的工具与技能演化可在冻结基础模型的前提下有效实现遥感智能体的持续自我改进，并显著优于现有方法；能力缺口弥补与经验跨任务迁移是性能增益的关键来源。

Abstract: We are interested in developing a continually self-improving remote sensing agentic system. It should identify and address its capability gaps, retaining the resulting improvements to enhance performance on subsequent tasks. However, existing systems typically rely on fixed toolsets and confine their experience accumulation to re-orchestrating predefined capabilities, constraining both capability expansion and the transfer of improvements across tasks. As the infrastructure for agentic systems, the harness manages tools and workflow knowledge as editable, persistent resources outside the base model. These resources can therefore be updated and reused across tasks without additional post-training. Based on these observations, we propose RemoteEvolver, an external harness for remote sensing agents that drives tool and workflow evolution through execution feedback. With the base model parameters fixed, RemoteEvolver acquires missing specialized tools on demand and distills verified successful execution experience from individual tasks into agent skills that can be reused across tasks. The acquired tools and accumulated skills jointly support planning and execution on subsequent tasks. Experiments on Earth-Bench and OpenEarthAgent-Bench show that RemoteEvolver achieves average accuracy gains of 8.18 and 23.86 percentage points over its initial state when tools are fully available and partially missing. Under the same settings, it also outperforms the state-of-the-art methods by an average of 6.77 and 17.10 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [44] [Analyzing Multi-Layer Visual Representations of Large Vision-Language Models for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27128)
*Satoshi Hashimoto,Hitoshi Nishimura,Mori Kurokawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 分析LVLM多层视觉表示，比较逐层特征与多层融合在异常检测中的有效性，给出面向视觉中心任务的表示选择经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM通过projector将视觉编码器特征接入语言模型，深层特征偏高层语义且与文本对齐，浅层与中间层保留局部外观、细粒度形状等视觉中心信息；下游任务性质不同，需据此选择层表示与融合方式，而此问题缺乏系统实证。

Method: 以异常检测为下游任务，对多层视觉表示进行逐层特征与多层融合的对比；覆盖多种监督级别与多种分类器，并从多个视角考察多层表示对性能的影响，分析性能差异的来源。

Result: 通过对比逐层特征与多层融合在不同监督级别和分类器下的性能差异，识别各层表示对异常检测的贡献，并归纳出表示选择与融合策略的有效性规律。

Conclusion: 多层视觉表示对异常检测的贡献存在层次差异，应按任务性质选择层表示与融合方式；为视觉中心任务的表示选择提供了实证性经验与指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze multi-layer visual representations in large vision-language models (LVLMs) and examine the effectiveness of layer-wise features and multi-layer fusion for anomaly detection. Representative LVLMs connect visual encoder features to a language model through a projector. In this design, deep visual encoder features carry high-level semantics and alignment with text representations, whereas shallow and intermediate layers retain information relevant to vision-centric tasks, such as local appearance and fine-grained shape. It is therefore important to choose layer representations and fusion methods according to the nature of the downstream task. Focusing on anomaly detection, we compare layer-wise features and multi-layer fusion across multiple supervision levels and a variety of classifiers, and examine from several perspectives how multi-layer visual representations affect downstream performance. By analyzing the resulting performance differences, we further discuss the contribution of each layer representation to anomaly detection and provide empirical insights into representation selection for vision-centric tasks.

</details>


### [45] [Crafting the Difference, Grounding the Evidence: Discriminative Concept Learning for Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27135)
*Siyu Zhu,Feng Ding,Zixiao Jin,Kaizhi Li,Xin Dong,Haisheng Fu,Yuchen Zou,Guohe Zhang,Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT框架，通过概念精炼（难负样本条件生成+对比打分+Unique-QDPP去冗余）与特征接地token，将LLM文本概念与骨架特征显式耦合，在NTU-60/NTU-120等数据集上提升零样本与广义零样本骨架动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的描述能丰富零样本骨架动作识别，但存在两点缺陷：一是描述重复包含易混淆动作共有的线索，缺乏判别性；二是上下文语义与骨架特征之间缺乏显式关联。

Method: CRAFT由两部分构成：(1) Concept Refinement——以难负样本为条件进行文本生成，并用对比打分筛选出能区分该动作与其最相近竞争类别的概念；再以Unique-QDPP在语义槽内选择互补描述、阻止跨槽复用，构建概念库。(2) Feature-grounded Tokens——用该概念库监督可学习的概念token，使其从每个骨架中读取运动与上下文线索，实现文本语义与骨架特征的显式对齐。

Result: 在固定的NTU-60与NTU-120划分上，CRAFT在4种识别范式的5个基线方法上均带来提升；ZSL准确率与GZSL调和平均在每个"基线-划分"组合上均上升，平均分别提升5.4与5.9个百分点；随机划分实验将增益扩展至三个数据集（含PKU-MMD）。

Conclusion: 通过精炼LLM概念的判别性与互补性并将其接地到骨架特征，可稳定、跨范式地提升零样本及广义零样本骨架动作识别，方法具备较好的泛化性。

Abstract: LLM-generated descriptions enrich zero-shot skeleton-based action recognition but repeat cues shared by confusable actions. Their contextual semantics also lack explicit links to skeleton features. To address these challenges, we propose CRAFT, which couples Concept Refinement and Feature-grounded Tokens. Hard-negative-conditioned generation and contrastive scoring identify concepts that distinguish each action from its closest rivals. Unique-QDPP selects complementary descriptions within each semantic slot and prevents their reuse across slots. This bank supervises learnable concept tokens that read motion and context cues from each skeleton. On fixed NTU-60 and NTU-120 splits, CRAFT improves five baselines spanning four recognition paradigms. ZSL accuracy and GZSL harmonic mean increase on every baseline-split pair, by 5.4 and 5.9 percentage points on average. Random-split experiments extend these gains to three datasets, including PKU-MMD. Code will be released after acceptance.

</details>


### [46] [TrajRealm: A Large-Scale Point Tracking Dataset with Diverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27136)
*Ni Yang,Weiguang Zhao,Jiafei Cao,Yankai Jiang,Haoyu Guo,Tao Yang,Chunhua Shen,Junting Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个达千万帧规模的公开合成点跟踪数据集 TrajRealm，源自游戏与具身仿真，提供 RGB、深度、相机参数及持久 2D/3D 点轨迹标注，并构建 8 个子类的 TrajRealm-Bench，评测并提升现有跟踪器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用点跟踪需要大规模、动态多样且几何精确的训练数据，但现有数据集难以同时兼顾丰富的动态内容、准确的场景几何与足够的规模。

Method: 利用现代游戏的丰富环境与运动系统采集多样目标、活动与交互，并结合具身仿真补充机器人操作与室内导航数据，构建千万帧级合成点跟踪数据集；标注包含 RGB 观测、深度、相机参数以及前景物体与静态环境表面上持久且几何一致的 2D/3D 点轨迹；同时构建含 Interaction 与 Locomotion 两大类、共八个子类别的 TrajRealm-Bench。

Result: 基准评测揭示了现有跟踪器在复杂运动处理与长期点对应维持方面的优势与局限；在 TrajRealm 上训练可提升现有跟踪方法在合成与真实基准上的性能。

Conclusion: TrajRealm 以千万帧规模、几何一致的稠密轨迹标注和配套基准，为通用点跟踪提供了可扩展的训练与评测基础，验证了合成数据对真实场景跟踪的有效迁移。

Abstract: General-purpose point tracking requires large-scale training data with diverse dynamics, yet existing datasets struggle to combine rich dynamic content, accurate scene geometry, and sufficient scale. We introduce TrajRealm, the first public synthetic point-tracking dataset to reach the ten-million-frame scale, constructed from games and embodied simulations. TrajRealm uses the rich environments and motion systems of modern games to capture diverse targets, activities, and interactions across diverse worlds, while embodied simulations add robot manipulation and indoor navigation data. The dataset provides RGB observations, depth, camera parameters, and persistent 2D and 3D point trajectories. These annotations provide precise, geometrically consistent supervision across foreground objects and static environmental surfaces. We also build TrajRealm-Bench with eight subcategories under Interaction and Locomotion to evaluate tracking across diverse scenes. Benchmark evaluations reveal the strengths and limitations of existing trackers in handling complex motion and maintaining long-term point correspondences. Training on TrajRealm improves the performance of existing tracking methods on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [47] [How Does SAM-3 Adapt? Capacity, Update Geometry, and Domain Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27142)
*Allan Kazakov,Abdurrahman Javat,Duygu Cakir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统比较五种SAM-3微调方案（LoRA、HiRA、KRAdapter、QuanTA、全量微调）在医学、遥感、材料稠密分割五数据集上的表现，发现紧凑PEFT常可媲美全量微调；但性能无法用名义秩或可训练参数量解释，SVD显示全量更新谱可压缩，而成功PEFT并非复现同一更新方向，且同域性能相近的方法在跨域迁移中响应不同，表明适配处于容量饱和状态、竞争力来自方法特异的更新路径而非单一低秩逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示式分割基础模型正被用于专业领域，但SAM-3在超出原始训练分布时应如何微调尚无定论；尤其不清楚紧凑参数高效微调能否替代全量微调，以及其性能来源的机制解释缺失。

Method: 在五个覆盖医学影像、遥感与稠密材料分割的数据集上，横向比较LoRA、HiRA、KRAdapter、QuanTA与全量微调五类适配方法；以DSC与IoU评估同域性能，并对更新矩阵做SVD谱分析，结合LoveDA Urban/Rural域迁移实验考察分布偏移下的行为差异。

Result: 紧凑PEFT在DSC与IoU上常匹配或接近全量微调；性能与名义适配器秩、可训练参数量无单调对应关系。SVD表明全量微调更新谱具有可压缩性，但成功PEFT集中于不同的更新分量与子空间，并非恢复相同方向；同域性能相近的方法在域迁移下表现分化。

Conclusion: SAM-3适配在实践中常处于容量饱和状态；有竞争力的微调源于方法特异的更新路径，而非对全量微调的单一低秩近似，因此选型不应仅依据秩或参数量，还需考察更新谱结构与跨域鲁棒性。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation foundation models are increasingly used in specialized domains, but it remains unclear how SAM-3 should be fine-tuned when adapting it beyond its original training distribution. We study this question by comparing five adaptation families --LoRA, HiRA, KRAdapter, QuanTA, and Full Fine-Tuning-- across five segmentation datasets spanning medical imaging, remote sensing, and dense material segmentation. Across these datasets, compact parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods often match or approach Full Fine-Tuning in DSC and IoU, suggesting that strong in-domain adaptation does not always require updating the full model. However, this performance cannot be explained by nominal adapter rank or trainable parameter count alone. To investigate why different methods perform similarly, we analyze their update spectra and their behavior under LoveDA Urban/Rural transfer. Our SVD analysis shows that Full Fine-Tuning updates are spectrally compressible. However, successful PEFT methods do not simply recover the same update directions; instead, they often concentrate changes in different components and subspaces. The domain-transfer study further shows that methods with similar in-domain performance can respond differently under distribution shift. Together, these results suggest that SAM-3 adaptation is often capacity-saturated in practice, while competitive fine-tuning arises through method-specific update paths rather than a single low-rank approximation to Full Fine-Tuning.

</details>


### [48] [Real-Anchored Complementary Evidence for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27161)
*Wenpeng Mu,Tanfeng Sun,Zhenyu Shao,Qiang Xu,Xinghao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RACE框架，仅用真实图像训练，融合全局与局部互补证据检测生成图像，在6个基准上平均AUROC达96.59%，5个域外基准达97.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型不断演进，基于生成样本训练的检测器易依赖特定生成器的伪影线索，泛化性差；而真实照片共享源于物理成像与采集过程的规律性，可作为跨生成器的共同参照。

Method: RACE仅使用真实训练图像：全局通路结合冻结特征与可学习表征评估与真实参照的接近度，并引入对受控扰动的响应以补充表征敏感性；局部通路以中间特征和带符号扰动响应为上下文重建完整痕迹描述子，检测证据仅限静态终端残差的重建误差；最后用真实图像统计量对齐并融合全局与局部得分，在独立真实集上校准固定决策阈值。

Result: 在六个基准中平均AUROC最高，为96.59%；五个域外基准上达97.18%；对图像扰动及真实图像来源变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 真实图像规律性可作为跨多种生成器与成像条件的可迁移检测基础。

Abstract: Evolving generative models challenge detector generalization, as methods trained on generated examples may rely on generator-specific cues. By contrast, authentic photographs share regularities rooted in physical image formation and acquisition, providing a common reference beyond individual generators. Building on these regularities, we propose Real-Anchored Complementary Evidence (RACE), which learns complementary global and local detection evidence using only authentic training images. First, the global pathway combines frozen and learned representations to assess proximity to authentic references. Next, it incorporates responses to controlled perturbations, complementing static compatibility with representation sensitivity. The local pathway then reconstructs complete trace descriptors, using intermediate features and signed perturbation responses as context while restricting detection evidence to static terminal-residual reconstruction errors. Finally, authentic-image statistics align the global and local scores for fusion, and an independent authentic set calibrates a decision threshold held fixed across test generators. Across six benchmarks, RACE achieves the highest average AUROC of 96.59% among the compared methods, reaching 97.18% on five out-of-domain benchmarks. Together with robustness to image perturbations and variation in authentic-image sources, these results support authentic-image regularities as a transferable basis for detection across diverse generators and image conditions.

</details>


### [49] [PhysForce: Physics-aware Force Control for Interactive Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27164)
*Xuancheng Xu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yue Ma,Zheng Zhu,Bing-Kun Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysForce 是一个两阶段（监督微调+强化学习）框架，通过将力语义编码进潜空间、建立力-潜状态变化的归因关联，并用自监督奖励提升物理一致性与泛化性，改进交互式视频世界模型对施力的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型以运动轨迹表示力，会引入不当运动先验，使模型过度依赖位移模式；且其内部表征无法可靠定位力引起的状态变化，难以将所施加力与物理响应关联；同时力标注数据稀缺，导致未见场景下物理一致性与时序稳定性不足。

Method: 两阶段框架：监督微调阶段包括（1）Latent Force Intervention Encoder，将力的位置、方向、幅值映射到视频潜空间且不暴露未来运动；（2）Physics Attribution Network，将力干预与潜状态的局部变化关联，强化物理响应学习。强化学习阶段采用（3）Physics-Aware Reinforcement Learning，在分布外视频上使用自监督奖励。

Result: 大量实验表明，PhysForce 在力响应性、物理一致性和泛化性上均有提升，验证了其对交互式视频世界模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 以力为中心的表征与归因机制结合自监督强化学习，可有效缓解轨迹先验依赖与标注数据不足问题，为可交互、物理一致的视频世界模型提供可行路径。

Abstract: Interactive video world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve in response to user interventions, including physically consistent state changes induced by applied forces. Existing methods often represent forces as motion trajectories, which may introduce inappropriate motion priors, causing the model to over-rely on encoded displacement patterns. More fundamentally, the model's internal representations do not reliably localize force-induced state changes, making it difficult to associate applied forces with their resulting physical responses. Also, limited force-annotated data make it difficult to produce physically consistent and temporally stable predictions in unseen scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose PhysForce, a two-stage framework that integrates supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning through three complementary components. During supervised fine-tuning, (1) the Latent Force Intervention Encoder maps force location, direction, and magnitude into the video latent space without exposing future motion; and (2) the Physics Attribution Network associates force interventions with localized changes in the latent state, strengthening the learning of physical responses to applied forces. During reinforcement learning, (3) Physics-Aware Reinforcement Learning uses self-supervised rewards on out-of-distribution videos to improve physical consistency and generalization. Extensive experiments show that PhysForce improves force responsiveness, physical consistency, and generalization, demonstrating its effectiveness for interactive video world models.

</details>


### [50] [Mem-X: A Memory-Dependent Cross-Episode Benchmark for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27166)
*Zhikang Shi,Yuk Tung Samuel Fang,Hongyu Ding,Zixuan Chen,Yujin Wang,Junhui Guo,You Wu,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mem-X 是一个评估跨回合记忆依赖型 VLN 的基准，含 2,071 回合、710 会话、34 场景，并提供 MemBridge 模块化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨回合 VLN 基准难以区分成功导航是依赖历史记忆还是当前探索，缺少对记忆依赖性的明确评测。

Method: 将回合组织为会话，后续指令引用早期回合出现的物体或路线；设三级任务：历史物体回忆、记忆内定位与情节索引、记忆外重定位与历史路线复现；后两级仅给回合内位姿、无跨帧变换，需建立与历史记忆的空间对应。另提出 MemBridge 基线，融合语义场景记忆、跨回合定位与由粗到细检索。

Result: 共 1,204 个记忆依赖回合；SOTA VLN 方法实验显示 MemBridge 带来收益，同时记忆依赖导航仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: Mem-X 与 MemBridge 为记忆依赖型具身导航提供了明确评测与可插拔基线，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables embodied agents to reuse past observations across tasks. However, existing cross-episode vision-and-language navigation (VLN) benchmarks often leave it unclear whether successful navigation relies on historical memory or current exploration. We introduce Mem-X, a benchmark designed to evaluate memory-dependent cross-episode VLN. Mem-X organizes episodes into sessions in which subsequent instructions refer to objects or routes encountered in earlier episodes. Its three levels evaluate historical-object recall, in-memory localization and episodic indexing, and off-memory relocalization and historical-route reproduction. For the latter two levels, agents receive episode-local poses but no transformations between episode frames, requiring them to establish spatial correspondence with historical memory. Mem-X comprises 2,071 episodes across 710 sessions in 34 scenes, including 1,204 memory-dependent episodes. We also provide MemBridge, a modular baseline that combines semantic scene memory, cross-episode localization, and coarse-to-fine retrieval to support existing VLN agents. Experiments with state-of-the-art VLN methods demonstrate the benefits of MemBridge while revealing substantial room for improvement on memory-dependent navigation. The benchmark and baseline code are available at https://github.com/NJU-R-L-Group-Embodied-Lab/Mem-X.

</details>


### [51] [FedQCal: Quadratic Programming with Dual-Space Calibration for One-Shot Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27170)
*Shunsei Koshibuchi,Hiroshi Kera,Kazuhiko Kawamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedQCal：用服务器参考模型定义对齐要求，以最小范数QP求解聚合系数，并通过分类损失双重变量选择合并点，实现异构客户端模型的一次性聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮联邦学习只能在一次上传后合并异构客户端模型；各客户端本地类分布不同，难以判断应保留哪些客户端更新。

Method: 以服务器小规模有标注集训练参考模型，给出聚合的对齐要求；用最小范数二次规划（QP）在满足对齐约束下避免系数过大；通过对相同服务器样本的分类损失确定两个对偶变量以选择合并点，从而无需预先设定对齐阈值；理论上证明所选系数在其诱导阈值下仍为精确QP解，并给出系数范数限制投影误差放大的扰动界。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100与SVHN上多数设置优于单轮FedAvg；消融实验支持参考引导加权与系数校准，即使每类仅有一张有标注服务器图像仍有效。

Conclusion: 参考模型引导的约束式QP聚合可作为单轮异构联邦学习的有效方案，且对服务器标注数据需求极低。

Abstract: One-shot federated learning must combine heterogeneous client models after a single upload. Different local class distributions make it difficult to decide which client updates to retain. We propose FedQCal, which uses a reference model trained on a small labeled server set to define alignment requirements for aggregation. The key idea is to retain sufficient reference alignment while avoiding unnecessarily large aggregation coefficients. A minimum-norm quadratic program (QP) enforces these requirements. Classification loss on the same server examples selects a merge through two dual variables, so the alignment thresholds need not be prescribed in advance. We prove that the selected coefficients remain exact QP solutions at their induced thresholds. A perturbation bound explains how coefficient norm limits the amplification of projection errors. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and SVHN show gains over one-round FedAvg in most settings. Ablations support reference-guided weighting and coefficient calibration, even with one labeled server image per class.

</details>


### [52] [GEG-SAM: Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model for Weakly Supervised Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27183)
*Ao Wang,Lei Zhang,Lituan Wang,Zhenwei Zhang,Xiangbao Gong,Zhiyi Zhang,Shunyao Wang,Xianglei Yuan,Zizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GEG-SAM，利用内镜医师注视轨迹的结构化关系与对应视觉线索，弱监督调制冻结SAM实现息肉分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将注视点视为与目标直接关联的孤立空间提示，忽略了自然注视是连续视觉搜索过程：注视点在时间上相连、空间上有组织，并与视觉内容耦合。仅按局部视觉内容独立解释每个注视点，遗漏了注视轨迹的时间演化与空间组织；仅建模注视点间关系又无法揭示其所关注区域的视觉语义。

Method: GEG-SAM框架：1）建模注视探索的时间动态与空间拓扑，捕获结构化注视关系；2）将关系表示与注视对齐的视觉上下文关联，得到注视条件化token；3）将这些token注入冻结的SAM编码器，调制其预训练图像特征，引导SAM向病灶导向分割。

Result: 在五个公开息肉分割数据集上验证有效性，平均Dice较最强弱监督基线提升9.30个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式利用注视探索中的结构化关系与视觉线索可有效调制SAM，为弱监督息肉分割提供原则性框架，并显著优于现有弱监督方法。

Abstract: Endoscopist gaze offers an unobtrusively acquired record of lesion-oriented visual exploration during routine clinical inspection, providing relevant supervision for tasks. However, existing methods treat fixations as isolated spatial prompts directly associated with the target, overlooking the fact that natural gaze records a continuous visual search process in which fixations are temporally connected, spatially organized, and coupled with the visual content. Interpreting each fixation independently based only on its local visual content overlooks the temporal evolution and spatial organization of the gaze trajectory, whereas modeling relations among fixations alone cannot reveal the visual semantics of the regions they attend to. To this end, we propose the Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model (GEG-SAM), a principled weakly supervised framework designed to explicitly exploit structured gaze relations and their corresponding visual cues to modulate SAM for polyp segmentation. Specifically, GEG-SAM captures structured gaze relations by modeling the temporal dynamics and spatial topology of gaze exploration. It then associates these relational representations with fixation-aligned visual context to derive gaze-conditioned tokens, which are further injected into the frozen SAM encoder to modulate its pretrained image features. In this way, GEG-SAM effectively exploits the rich information embedded in gaze exploration to guide SAM toward lesion-oriented segmentation. Extensive experiments on five public polyp segmentation datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, improving the mean Dice by 9.30 percentage points over the strongest weakly supervised baseline.

</details>


### [53] [SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27187)
*Xiao Lin,Yaoxu Lyu,Ronghao Dang,Zhili Liu,Kaijing Ma,Yang Zhou,Dingning Liu,Runzhe Teng,Jun Cen,Shanghang Zhang,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SCAP（Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining）预训练阶段：在进入VLM前对视觉与文本token随机掩码，既抑制视觉捷径又保留视觉-语言联合建模通路，在四种VLA架构上稳定提升性能，真实机器人三任务平均成功率达76.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模态动作专家预训练虽能控制视觉捷径的竞争，却会损害后续联合预测所需的视觉-语言交互；作者用诊断模型证明该通路一旦被禁用，即便拥有完整视觉与语言输入、无限适配训练也无法恢复。

Method: 构建可禁用联合视觉-语言建模通路的统一诊断模型；提出SCAP：在完整输入训练之前加入动作专家预训练阶段，随机掩码送入VLM的视觉与文本token。

Result: 在该诊断模型中，SCAP能在预训练及后续完整输入训练中保留联合视觉-语言建模通路；相较直接完整输入训练，降低对视觉捷径的依赖、提升对语言指令的依赖；四种VLA架构均稳定提升；真实机器人三任务平均成功率76.67%，比完整输入预训练高25个百分点。

Conclusion: 随机上下文掩码是对抗视觉捷径并保持视觉-语言联合能力的有效预训练策略，可作为通用VLA适配前置阶段。

Abstract: Single-modality action-expert pretraining controls competition from visual shortcuts before vision--language--action (VLA) adaptation. Yet single-modality pretraining can impair the vision--language interactions needed for subsequent joint prediction. We construct a diagnostic model in which VA or LA pretraining can disable an initially active pathway for joint vision--language modeling. In this model, subsequent training with complete visual and language inputs cannot restore the pathway, even with unlimited adaptation. We therefore introduce Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining (SCAP), a new action-expert pretraining stage that precedes full-input training. During this stage, visual and text tokens are randomly masked before entering the VLM. In this unified diagnostic model, SCAP can preserve the joint vision--language modeling pathway during pretraining and subsequent full-input training. Compared with direct full-input training, it can also reduce reliance on visual shortcuts and increase reliance on language instructions. SCAP consistently improves performance across four different VLA architectures. In real-robot experiments, it achieves 76.67% average success across three instruction-conditioned manipulation tasks, outperforming full-input pretraining by 25 percentage points.

</details>


### [54] [DepthMSA: Combinatorial Training and Budget-Aware Depth Allocation for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27198)
*Pan Wang,Jie Peng,Zhimin Li,Jingxian Chen,Hui Ji,Huanrui Yang,Pengfei Zhou,Tianlong Chen,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DepthMSA，一个预算感知的多模态情感分析框架，可为不同模态分配异构编码深度，通过组合式随时训练与推理时成本感知分配器，在接近全深度精度下大幅降低FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSA模型使用大型预训练编码器，但无论计算成本或预测贡献如何，都将每个模态处理到全深度。模态编码器计算成本与更深处理的预测收益之间存在显著不匹配，因此需研究如何在模态间分配有限计算，同时保证异构深度表示仍能有效融合。

Method: DepthMSA在单一模型中支持灵活模态深度；引入组合式随时训练（combinatorial anytime training），用一次全深度编码器前向监督融合头覆盖所有跨模态深度组合；推理时使用免训练、成本感知的分配器，在样本级FLOPs预算下选择验证集校准误差最低的配置；另训练带跳过连接的变体。

Result: CMU-MOSEI上，DepthMSA与全深度模型精度差距在1个百分点内，实测FLOPs减少45%；带跳过连接变体减少82%。当CLIP视觉编码器主导计算时，分配偏向降低视觉深度，精度不变下FLOPs减少56%。相比需为每个预算训练控制器的ADMN，跳过变体以3.2倍更少FLOPs和更低延迟达到相同精度，且无需按预算训练。

Conclusion: 异构模态深度是提升MSA计算效率的有效途径，配合组合式随时训练与成本感知分配，可在几乎不损失精度的情况下显著降低计算量，并优于按预算训练的层分配方法。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) models rely on large pretrained encoders, yet typically process every modality to its full depth, regardless of its computational cost or predictive contribution. This raises a fundamental question: how should limited computation be allocated across modalities to preserve predictive performance? We reveal a substantial mismatch between the computational cost of modality encoders and the predictive benefit of processing them more deeply, suggesting that different modalities require different encoding depths. However, enabling heterogeneous depths introduces a key challenge: representations extracted at different depths must still be effectively fused. To address this, we propose $\textbf{DepthMSA}$, a budget-aware framework that supports flexible modality depths within a single MSA model. Specifically, we introduce combinatorial anytime training, which supervises the fusion head across all cross-modal depth combinations using a single full-depth encoder pass. At inference, a training-free, cost-aware allocator selects the configuration with the lowest validation-calibrated error under a sample-specific FLOPs budget. On CMU-MOSEI, DepthMSA maintains accuracy within one percentage point of the full-depth model while reducing measured FLOPs by 45\%, or by 82\% with a separately trained skip-enabled variant. When a CLIP visual encoder dominates computation, the allocation shifts toward reducing visual depth, achieving 56\% fewer FLOPs at unchanged accuracy. Compared with ADMN, an adaptive layer-allocation method that trains a controller for each budget, the skip-enabled variant reaches its accuracy with 3.2$\times$ fewer FLOPs and lower latency, without any per-budget training. Our project is available at https://pwang322.github.io/DepthMSA/.

</details>


### [55] [Backbone-Specific LoRA Geometry: Orthogonality, Superposition, and Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27201)
*Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Keigo Watanabe,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究跨预训练骨干共享LoRA更新的条件，提出以骨干特有的更新方向作为"训练骨干签名"，并基于余弦相似度实现骨干识别与注册适配器复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用一个共享的LoRA更新服务于多个预训练模型可简化部署，但组合方法与跨模型迁移两条路线各有局限；跨骨干共享更新时，能否在保持精度的同时区分各更新对对应骨干的贡献，其条件尚不明确。

Method: 在高斯独立均匀初始化下利用注意力对称性与等变LoRA学习推导跨骨干相关性界；用局部梯度流模型解释同骨干同任务独立训练间正的平均相关；据此设计基于余弦的骨干识别与内积检验，在无需坐标对齐的情况下复用注册适配器。

Result: 在CLIP骨干上的实验表明，共享求和适配器平均精度与各自独立适配器几乎相同；余弦分数在评估任务上能可靠识别源骨干；不同骨干适配器求和后，注册适配器仍可被检测到。

Conclusion: 骨干特有的更新方向可作为可辨识签名，为跨骨干LoRA共享、适配器溯源与复用提供了理论依据和无需坐标对齐的实用检验方法。

Abstract: Sharing one low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update across pretrained models simplifies deployment. Composition methods typically combine updates from a common backbone, while cross-model transfer transforms updates for a target backbone. The conditions for sharing updates across backbones while preserving accuracy and distinguishing individual contributions remain unclear. Our idea is to use backbone-specific update directions as signatures of their training backbones. We derive a cross-backbone correlation bound from attention symmetries and equivariant LoRA learning under independent, uniform pretrained feature orientations. A local gradient-flow model explains positive mean correlation between independent runs on the same backbone and task. These patterns motivate cosine-based backbone identification and inner-product tests for registered-adapter reuse without coordinate alignment. Experiments on CLIP backbones show that shared sums achieve nearly the same mean accuracy as separate adapters. Cosine scores reliably identify source backbones across the evaluated tasks. Registered adapters remain detectable in sums of adapters from different backbones.

</details>


### [56] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Qwen3-VL for Handwritten Mathematical Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27208)
*Manh Tuan Ha,Xuan Bao Viet Tran,Vo Minh Dat Nguyen,Minh Nhat Vo,Quoc Trung Nguyen,Van Bay Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在有限算力下对4-bit量化的Qwen3-VL-4B施加rank-16 LoRA，将手写数学表达式识别（HMER）建模为线性化符号标签图（SLG）的多模态结构生成，并在CROHME 2019上取得43.74%完全匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HMER需同时恢复符号身份与二维语法，局部看似合理的转写仍可能结构错误；同时希望验证紧凑视觉语言模型在有限计算资源下能否胜任该任务。

Method: 以4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B为起点，对视觉、语言、注意力与MLP模块施加rank-16 LoRA，用指令格式的CROHME 2019样本训练，生成线性化SLG；对照内部实现的ViT-Transformer基线、零样本Qwen3-VL及若干内部替代方案，并进行增加2,000条人工书写、公式引导样本的数据组成消融。

Result: 在CROHME 1,198条有效测试样本上，CROHME-only模型获得524例完全匹配（43.74%）、语料BLEU-4为77.67；任务专用ViT基线为4.92%与43.26；加入2,000条人工样本的消融反而将完全匹配降至42.32%。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是给出一个有证据支撑、资源高效地将HMER表述为多模态结构生成的方法，配合受控的数据组成消融，并分析了局部token重叠与全局结构正确性之间的残余差距；额外数据未带来自动增益，反映了域对齐问题。

Abstract: Handwritten mathematical expression recognition (HMER) requires joint recovery of symbol identity and two-dimensional syntax, so a locally plausible transcription may still be structurally wrong. We study whether a compact vision-language model can be adapted to generate linearized Symbol Label Graphs (SLGs) under limited computing resources. Starting from a 4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B checkpoint, we apply rank-16 Low-Rank Adaptation to the vision, language, attention, and MLP modules and train with instruction-formatted CROHME 2019 examples. We compare this model with an internally implemented ViT-Transformer baseline, a zero-shot Qwen3-VL configuration, and additional internally evaluated alternatives. On the 1,198 valid CROHME test examples, the CROHME-only model obtains 524 exact matches (43.74%) and a corpus BLEU-4 score of 77.67, compared with 4.92% and 43.26 for the task-specific ViT baseline. An ablation that adds 2,000 manually written, formula-guided samples decreases exact match to 42.32%, exposing a domain-alignment issue rather than an automatic benefit from more data. The principal contribution is therefore an evidence-backed, resource-efficient formulation of HMER as multimodal structural generation, together with a controlled data-composition ablation and an analysis of the remaining gap between local token overlap and globally correct structure.

</details>


### [57] [EAST: Event-Anchored Surface Transport for Event-Based Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27224)
*Ke Luo,Wei Zhai,Yuliang Wu,Pengfei Yu,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EAST，将事件约束的SDF与高斯渲染耦合，解决事件监督高斯重建中外观变化补偿深度误差导致的表面歧义问题，提升表面精度并支持高效网格提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率与宽动态范围，显式高斯渲染效率高，二者结合有前景。但连续表面恢复困难：渲染外观的变化可以补偿深度误差，同时仍保持预测的亮度变化一致，使深度-外观解耦不足、几何病态。

Method: EAST由两部分耦合：(1) 事件约束的SDF，利用三角化支持的候选点锚定零水平集，并用事件时序测得的光流（normal flow）约束光线-表面交点深度；(2) 高斯中心从"实时场与冻结初始参考场"的描述子差异中获得有界修正。该修正初始为零，但可将图像梯度传导至实时场，从而实现SDF与高斯的联合优化。

Result: 在PAEv3D与NeRF Synthetic上，相比高斯基线，表面精度与参考视角渲染质量均提升，并能从学习到的场中高效提取网格。

Conclusion: 以事件锚定的表面传输机制可将几何约束显式注入高斯重建，缓解深度-亮度歧义，在保持高斯渲染效率的同时获得更准确的连续表面与便捷网格化。

Abstract: Event-supervised Gaussian reconstruction combines the high temporal resolution and wide dynamic range of event cameras with efficient explicit rendering. However, recovering a continuous surface remains difficult: changes in rendered appearance can compensate for depth errors while preserving the predicted brightness changes. We introduce Event-Anchored Surface Transport (EAST), which couples an event-constrained signed distance field to Gaussian rendering. Triangulation-supported candidates anchor the zero set, while normal flow measured from event timing constrains ray-surface intersection depths. Gaussian centers receive bounded corrections from the descriptor difference between the live field and a frozen initial reference. The correction is zero at initialization, yet transmits image gradients to the live field. Experiments on PAEv3D and NeRF Synthetic show improved surface accuracy and reference-view rendering over Gaussian baselines, together with efficient mesh extraction from the learned field.

</details>


### [58] [Prune to Expand: Training Prompt Enhancers with Less to Imitate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27229)
*Tianhe Wu,Qilin Sun,Kede Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现基于密集描述构建的提示增强监督对生成增益不敏感，提出文本端的 P2E 方法选择性删除冗余句子以精炼监督，从而提升对齐度与视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示增强（PE）研究常用密集描述派生出类用户的短提示来构建 SFT 配对，隐含假设"能改进图像生成的描述也能提供有效监督"；但作者发现该假设不成立，密集描述中存在与用户意图弱相关的冗余细节监督。

Method: P2E（Prune to Expand），一种纯文本方法：在每个样本的删除上限约束下，选择性删除密集描述中的冗余句子，同时保留显式用户需求与有信息量的视觉结构，从而精炼 SFT 监督信号。

Result: 在相同训练配置下，P2E 在多种图像类别与多个视觉生成器上，相比常规密集描述 SFT 均提升了与人类偏好的对齐度和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 以生成增益为依据构建的描述配对并非有效监督信号；通过剪枝冗余描述内容来精炼监督，是提升提示增强模型性能的简单有效途径。

Abstract: Prompt enhancement (PE) refines concise user prompts into detailed descriptions to improve the compositional aesthetics of generated images while preserving user intent. A common practice constructs supervised fine-tuning (SFT) pairs by deriving short, user-like prompts from dense captions, implicitly assuming that captions that improve image generation also provide effective supervision. In this paper, however, we uncover a counterintuitive finding: PE models trained on caption pairs with substantial generation gains perform comparably to those trained on pairs with only marginal gains. We argue that dense captions can impose redundant supervision by requiring PE models to reproduce image-specific details weakly related to user intent. Motivated by this discrepancy, we introduce Prune to Expand (P2E), a simple text-only method that refines dense-caption supervision by selectively removing redundant sentences under a per-example deletion bound while preserving explicit user requirements and informative visual structure. Extensive experiments under identical training configurations demonstrate that P2E improves alignment with human preferences and visual quality over conventional dense-caption SFT across diverse image categories and visual generators.

</details>


### [59] [WINSO: A Large-Size Very-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Dataset for Wind Turbine Detection and Solar Panel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27241)
*Di Wang,Ke Li,Zhaokun Li,Weiping Ni,Junzheng Wu,Yahu Liu,Quan Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WINSO，一个基于0.5–0.7 m甚高分辨率卫星影像的大规模风光设施基准数据集，涵盖10,962幅大尺寸影像、166,473个标注实例，并建立检测/分割/超高分/视觉语言模型的系统评测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风光设施空间清单对能源规划至关重要，卫星遥感是大范围获取手段，但高质量标注数据稀缺且缺乏系统评测协议，制约大尺寸影像上的可靠设施制图。

Method: 构建WINSO基准：影像GSD 0.5–0.7 m，10,962幅大尺寸影像（单幅达44,032×62,208像素），覆盖亚太多地、至少71,600 km²、共286.36 gigapixels；经人工标注与专家核验，风力机用水平框、光伏板用多边形掩膜，共166,473实例；评测SOTA检测与分割模型、专用超高分架构及视觉语言模型在不同推理策略下的表现，并在两个外部公开数据集上做跨数据集泛化实验。

Result: 建立了多类模型的基线结果；跨数据集评测显示WINSO训练模型具备较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: WINSO为自动化清洁能源设施制图提供标准化基准，推动清洁能源转型的广域监测。

Abstract: Wind and solar energy are central to the global transition toward net-zero emissions and sustainable development. Accurate spatial inventories of wind and solar facilities are essential for future energy planning. Satellite remote sensing provides a practical and efficient means to acquire such geospatial data at scale. However, reliable facility mapping from large-size satellite images remains constrained by the scarcity of carefully annotated datasets and the lack of systematic evaluation protocols. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{WINSO}, a large-scale wind and solar benchmark based on very-high-resolution (VHR) satellite imagery with ground sampling distances (GSDs) of 0.5--0.7\,m. WINSO comprises 10,962 large-size images sampled from diverse geographic locations across the Asia-Pacific region, covering at least 71,600\,$\text{km}^2$ and totaling 286.36 gigapixels, with individual image dimensions reaching 44,032$\times$62,208 pixels. Through manual annotation and expert verification, a total of 166,473 instances are labeled using horizontal bounding boxes (HBBs) for wind turbines and polygon masks for solar panels. To build baselines for clean-energy facility mapping, we evaluate state-of-the-art detection and segmentation models, specialized ultra-high-resolution (UHR) architectures, and vision-language models across different inference strategies. Furthermore, cross-dataset evaluations on two external public datasets illustrate the strong generalization capability of the WINSO. WINSO establishes a standardized benchmark to advance automated clean-energy mapping, paving the way for wide-area monitoring of the clean-energy transition.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Prompt Imitation: Capability-Aware Video Generation with Smaller Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27252)
*Ziqi Ding,Yuxin Cao,Kai Ye,Yaobo Yang,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“oracle control ladder”诊断框架，用逐级注入目标视频信息的方式为推理时方法建立经验性上行空间参照，并据此构建带预算的控制器选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时方法仅用偏好分数比较小模型与专有视频生成器的差距，无法量化究竟弥合了多少差距，缺少可解释的上行空间参照。

Method: 在954个片段上对固定学生模型逐级提供更丰富的目标信息（稠密描述→首帧→深度视频→二者结合），测量各保真度指标在每一级的差距闭合率；用项目反应模型预测各级对各片段的闭合效果，并作为预算约束下选择策略的先验。

Result: 稠密描述几乎不恢复外观、基本不恢复运动；首帧闭合约一半外观差距、十分之一运动差距；深度视频闭合约四分之三运动差距但不影响色彩；二者结合在四位教师模型上闭合64–94%的各保真度差距，第二个学生模型结论一致；项目反应模型留出AUC 0.76–0.95；控制免费时固定稠密控制级难以超越，控制计费时策略在等成本下优于固定级前沿。

Conclusion: 将“信息注入”作为可度量的头寸参照，可把推理时方法的效果从相对偏好转为差距闭合率，并为预算受限的自适应控制选择提供可行且稳健的依据。

Abstract: Inference-time methods that lift a small open video generator toward a proprietary one are compared through preference scores, which never say how much of the gap to a target video they close. We introduce the oracle control ladder, which hands a fixed student progressively richer information from a target clip and reads the closure of each fidelity metric at each rung as an empirical headroom reference for the methods that must synthesize that information. On 954 clips of the principal student, a dense caption reproduces little of the appearance and almost none of the motion of a target, a first frame closes half of the appearance gap and a tenth of the motion gap, a depth video closes three quarters of the motion gap and leaves color untouched, and the two together close 64 to 94% of every fidelity gap across four teachers, while a second student agrees on the rungs that it accepts. An item response model forecasts which rung closes which clip with held-out AUC of 0.76 to 0.95 on the fidelity criteria and serves as the prior of a budgeted selection policy. When controls are free, a fixed dense-control rung is hard to improve upon, and once controls are priced the policy lies above the fixed-rung frontier at matched cost and survives critics that ruin critic-driven search.

</details>


### [61] [EAGLE: Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27264)
*Zhongjian Zou,Quan Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EAGLE，一个即插即用框架，通过筛选视觉接地注意力头并结合分层证据组织，提升 MLLM 对高分辨率图像的细粒度理解，在 V*、HRBench-4K/8K 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在高分辨率图像细粒度理解上存在两大问题：不同注意力头对目标实体及其属性的接地可靠性差异大；局部细节与全局场景上下文割裂。

Method: EAGLE 为插件式框架：(1) 利用细粒度数据集筛选具有视觉接地能力的注意力头；(2) 实体感知接地，提取目标相关区域；(3) 构建分层视觉证据，并通过问题引导的路由选择最优视图组合；(4) 将选出的视图输入冻结的 MLLM。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 与 InternVL3-8B 两个骨干上均稳定提升性能；在 V*、HRBench-4K、HRBench-8K 上相比同骨干现有方法达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化接地信号选择与层次化证据组织，可在不训练骨干模型的前提下有效增强高分辨率图像细粒度理解，且具良好骨干通用性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still struggle with fine-grained understanding of high-resolution images: attention heads vary in their reliability for grounding target entities and their attributes, while localized details are often disconnected from the broader scene context. We propose EAGLE (Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding), a plug-in framework that jointly selects grounding signals and organizes hierarchical visual evidence. EAGLE employs a fine-grained dataset to screen visually-grounded attention heads, followed by entity-aware grounding to extract target-specific regions. It then integrates hierarchical evidence construction and question-guided routing to select optimal view combinations, feeding them into the frozen MLLM to enhance fine-grained visual understanding. Experiments with Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B demonstrate that EAGLE consistently improves performance across both backbones and achieves SOTA results on V*, HRBench-4K, and HRBench-8K compared with existing methods using the same backbone.

</details>


### [62] [PP-CFG: Polarity-Preserving Guidance for Controllable Few-Step Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27266)
*Junyi He,Xu Peng,Chengming Xu,Sixiao Zheng,Donghao Luo,Chao Wen,Peng Tang,Xijie Huang,Chengjie Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PP-CFG 通过在同一视觉骨干内以相反符号融合正/负文本条件的注意力响应，并引入 Scale-Time Polarity Mixer，使少步蒸馏扩散模型恢复可调 CFG 与运行时负提示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏显著降低采样成本，但常削弱可调无分类器引导（CFG）与运行时负提示能力；需要在不增加每步骨干前向次数的前提下恢复这一可控性。

Method: 提出 PP-CFG 架构：正、负文本条件保持分离，在共享视觉骨干内以相反符号组合其注意力响应；Scale-Time Polarity Mixer 依据引导尺度与去噪时间步对每个条件进行调制，使提示影响随生成过程变化；学生模型保留连续尺度输入与可选负提示，每步仅一次视觉骨干前向。在 Wan2.1（视频，DMD）与 Qwen-Image（图像，LCM 与 DMD）上验证。

Result: 四步生成取得 81.46 VBench，并在各引导尺度下较步蒸馏 DMD 具有更低的视频师生距离；在 NegElement-1K（1000 组配对图文/视频提示基准）上目标移除率高于对应教师模型。

Conclusion: 实验表明该方法在注意力架构、生成模态与蒸馏目标上均具通用性，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Abstract: Few-step diffusion distillation reduces sampling cost but often weakens adjustable classifier-free guidance (CFG) and runtime negative prompting. We introduce PP-CFG, an architecture that keeps positive and negative text conditions separate and combines their attention responses with opposite signs within a shared visual backbone. Its Scale-Time Polarity Mixer refines each condition using the guidance scale and denoising timestep, allowing prompt influence to vary throughout generation. The student retains a continuous scale input and selectable negative prompts with one visual backbone pass per step. We evaluate PP-CFG on Wan2.1 for video generation with distribution matching distillation (DMD), and on Qwen-Image with both latent consistency model (LCM) distillation and DMD. PP-CFG achieves 81.46 VBench in four steps and lower video teacher-student distances across guidance scales than step-distilled DMD. On NegElement-1K, our benchmark of 1,000 matched image and video prompt cases, it achieves higher target-removal rates than the corresponding teachers. These experiments support the method's generality across attention architectures, generation modalities, and distillation objectives while retaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [63] [Budgeted Safety Verification for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27268)
*Yuxin Cao,Ziqi Ding,Jiayu Zhang,Xianglin Yang,Wei Song,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 流式视频生成器边生成边发布，事后审核为时已晚。本文以"暴露量"（观众看到的有害帧数）为度量，在固定审核预算下，用生成器已算好的隐状态廉价打分，决定何时调用昂贵的视频语言模型检测器。在599条流上，无防御为81帧中46.25有害帧；逐块调用检测器降至0.91帧（每良性流评分80.1次），由守卫触发调用则为2.35帧（仅5.3次），随机分配预算仍为37.20帧，说明收益来自排序而非预算本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式发布模式下，"等到完整片段再判断"的审核机制在危害已被观看后才生效。核心问题不是"能否检测"，而是在有限审核预算下"把预算花在哪里"才能最小化观众实际看到的有害帧（暴露量）。

Method: 以暴露量为评价指标；利用生成器前向过程中已计算的隐状态训练轻量守卫，输出低成本分数以决定何时上调昂贵的视频语言模型检测器；与全量调用、随机预算分配、帧级检测器同角色替换等基线对比；并用独立审核模型及50人盲审评估一致性。

Result: 蒸馏因果生成器上599条流：无防御46.25/81有害帧；逐块视频模型0.91帧但每良性流评分80.1次；守卫触发2.35帧、5.3次；随机分配37.20帧；帧检测器同角色损失近6帧。独立模型与oracle一致度κ=0.74，盲审精确率96.1%、召回率82.8%。流越长排序收益越大，且可迁移到第二套系统。

Conclusion: 在流式视频安全中，预算的"排序/时机分配"比检测器本身更关键；必须将块作为片段整体判定而非逐帧判定；隐状态守卫可低成本、可迁移地显著降低有害暴露。

Abstract: A streamed video generator releases each chunk the moment that it is produced, so a safety decision that waits for the finished clip arrives after the harm has been seen. We measure a guardrail by exposure, the harmful frames a viewer is shown, and ask where a fixed moderation budget should be spent. The accurate detector reads decoded frames with a video language model and is too expensive for every chunk, so our guard reads hidden states the generator has computed already and spends a cheap score on deciding when to call it. On 599 streams from a distilled causal generator the undefended model shows 46.25 harmful frames of 81. The video model on every chunk leaves 0.91 frames at 80.1 scored per benign stream, and the same model triggered by our guard leaves 2.35 at 5.3. Spending that budget at random leaves 37.20 frames, so the gain is in the ordering, and a frame detector in the same role gives up almost six, so the judging stage must read the chunk as a clip. The ordering is worth more as the stream lengthens, and the guard carries to a second system. An independent guardrail model agrees with our oracle at $κ=0.74$, and a blinded audit by 50 participants puts its precision at 96.1% and recall at 82.8%. Warning. This paper contains examples of generated content that some readers may find disturbing or offensive in nature.

</details>


### [64] [MODULE: A Mutual-Promoting Deep Unfolding Framework Towards Degradation-Robust Multi-modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27269)
*Yunfei Deng,Han Xu,Jiayi Ma,Guangcan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出受理论启发的互促深度展开框架，将退化鲁棒多模态图像融合重构为联合优化问题，分解为迭代子问题并展开为多阶段网络，实现复原与融合的双向信息流，兼顾性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中的图像退化会削弱多模态图像融合效果，而现有退化鲁棒融合方法存在级联次优解（误差累积）和黑箱不可解释两大缺陷。

Method: 将退化鲁棒融合建模为联合优化问题，利用退化模型与模态生成机制显式刻画单任务先验与跨任务依赖；把多任务优化分解为任务相关的迭代子问题，建立复原与融合间双向互促信息流；再将该过程展开为多阶段深度网络，每个组件对应特定数学运算——融合后的跨模态先验正则化病态复原问题，净化后的模态特征反过来精炼融合输出。

Result: 大量实验表明该范式达到SOTA性能，同时具备更优的可解释性。

Conclusion: 基于模型与数据驱动相结合的统一展开框架，可透明地实现退化鲁棒多模态图像融合，避免级联次优与黑箱问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion is crucial for comprehensive scene representation, yet real-world degradations compromise its efficacy, necessitating degradation-robust fusion paradigms. Existing degradation-robust multi-modal image fusion methods are often hindered by cascaded sub-optimal solution or black-box opacity. To address these challenges, we propose a theory-inspired mutual-promoting deep unfolding framework. It reformulates degradation-robust image fusion as a joint optimization problem, utilizing the degradation model and modality generation mechanism to explicitly model both single-task priors and cross-task dependencies. By decomposing the complex multi-task optimization problem into task-related iterative subproblems, the framework establishes a bidirectional reciprocal information flow between restoration and fusion. This process is further unfolded into a multi-stage deep neural network, where each component explicitly corresponds to a specific mathematical operation. In our framework, the fused cross-modal prior actively regularizes the ill-posed restoration, while the purified modality features continuously refine the fusion output. It ensures a transparent architecture that combines the merits of both model-based and data-driven methodologies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing superior interpretability. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Meskend/MODULE.

</details>


### [65] [GAAFormer: Geometry-Anchored Attention Transformer for Joint Point Cloud Denoising and Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27281)
*Haoxi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GAAFormer：一个共享网络的几何锚定注意力八叉树Transformer，用同一模型统一完成点云去噪与上采样，在不增加参数的情况下同时改善CD、HD等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云去噪（从含噪观测恢复曲面）与上采样（从稀疏观测恢复曲面）都要求样本既能精确定位点、又能保持曲面覆盖。核心难点在于细化点位的同时维持覆盖度，且两类任务通常需要各自专用架构。

Method: 提出GAAFormer，统一的生成式八叉树Transformer，编码器-解码器共享。解码器将注意力邻域锚定到几何上，为占据预测与点生成提供空间上下文；采用固定体素窗口与交替平移，使存活节点的分组在输出树变化时保持一致。架构保持序列化注意力骨干的编码器、位置编码、预测头与参数量不变，并证明邻域规则的删除一致性（deletion-consistency）性质。

Result: PUNet上去噪平均CD为6.0643e-5，相对序列化骨干CD降低3.35%、HD降低19.34%。上采样在PU-GAN、PU1K、Sketchfab上CD分别为0.8475、1.7057、1.0147（×1e-5），在PU-GAN与Sketchfab上CD、HD、点到面距离均优于同一骨干。

Conclusion: 单一架构即可同时完成去噪与上采样；共享编码器-解码器中的同一几何锚定注意力模块在无需任务特定重设计、无需额外参数的前提下，同时提升去噪与稠密点生成效果。

Abstract: Point cloud denoising and upsampling require samples that accurately recover the underlying surface from noisy or sparse observations. A central challenge in both tasks is preserving surface coverage while refining point locations. We introduce GAAFormer (Geometry-Anchored Attention Transformer), a unified generative octree transformer that performs both tasks with a shared network. Its decoder anchors attention neighborhoods to geometry, providing spatial context for occupancy prediction and point generation. Fixed voxel windows and alternating shifts preserve the grouping of surviving nodes as the output tree changes. The design retains the encoder, positional encoding, prediction heads, and parameter count of a serialized-attention backbone. We establish a deletion-consistency property of the neighborhood rule and evaluate both tasks using the same model. On PUNet, GAAFormer achieves a mean Chamfer distance (CD) of $6.0643\times10^{-5}$, reducing CD and Hausdorff distance by 3.35% and 19.34%, respectively, relative to the serialized backbone. For upsampling, it achieves CD values of $0.8475$, $1.7057$, and $1.0147$ ($\times10^{-5}$) on PU-GAN, PU1K, and Sketchfab, respectively, with improvements in CD, Hausdorff distance, and point-to-surface distance over the same backbone on PU-GAN and Sketchfab. These results establish a single architecture for point cloud denoising and upsampling. Within the shared encoder-decoder, the same geometry-anchored attention module improves both noise removal and dense point generation without task-specific redesign or additional parameters.

</details>


### [66] [SIR-Agent: Skill-Augmented Image Restoration Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27285)
*Yijian Wang,Qingsen Yan,Weiyu Zhou,Mingde Zhang,Axi Niu,Haokui Zhang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIR-Agent，将图像复原知识存于可扩展技能库并以递归自我改进（RSI）实现技能库演化，避免微调MLLM导致的灾难性遗忘，在14个基准和7个未见任务上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像复原（IR）智能体依赖微调MLLM参数适配新IR任务，导致IR知识的灾难性遗忘。

Method: SIR-Agent将IR知识维护在可扩展技能库中；设计链反馈驱动的技能修订（CFSR），通过分析复原链识别并修订技能中的弱操作，并依据修订技能与已知任务的兼容性决定替换原技能或独立追加；进一步提出RSI驱动的技能元学习机制，识别CFSR的低效操作并自主优化其修订策略。

Result: 在14个IR基准（6种单一退化、8种混合退化场景）上取得更优的IR性能；在7个未见任务上的评估验证了对新IR任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以技能库+递归自我改进替代参数微调，可有效保留并演化IR知识，兼顾性能与泛化。

Abstract: Agents for Image Restoration (IR) aim to leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to autonomously perceive image degradation and invoke IR tools. However, existing IR agents typically rely on fine-tuning MLLM parameters to adapt to new IR tasks, leading to catastrophic forgetting of IR knowledge. To address this limitation, we propose a skill-augmented IR agent, dubbed SIR-Agent, that maintains IR knowledge in a scalable skill library and uses Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) to facilitate efficient skill-library evolution. Specifically, we design a Chain Feedback-driven Skill Revision (CFSR) to evolve the skill library. The CFSR analyzes restoration chains to identify and revise weak operations in existing skills. The compatibility of a revised skill with known tasks decides if it replaces the original skill or is appended independently. Furthermore, to advance CFSR's skill-library evolution, we present an RSI-driven skill meta-learning mechanism, which identifies CFSR's inefficient operations and autonomously refines its revision strategy. Extensive experiments across 14 IR benchmarks, covering 6 single-degradation and 8 mixed-degradation scenarios, demonstrate SIR-Agent's superior IR performance. Further evaluation on 7 unseen tasks validates SIR-Agent's generalization to new IR tasks.

</details>


### [67] [InterBand-EMG: A Cross-Band Foundation Model for Generalizable Hand-Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27289)
*Zongheng Guo,Tao Chen,Dalong Du,Yijian Yang,Heng Liu,Lun Shu,Chen Luo,Junhao Tu,Lei Shu,Mingzhou Wu,Yifan Liu,Yu Zhang,Zhenqiang Liu,Shilei Wen,Jie Zhou,Manuela Ferrario*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InterBand-EMG自监督预训练方法（频带量化+跨频带重建+结构化掩码码本预测），并发布SynapMyo八通道sEMG手势数据集（499用户、1227.74手时、68类动作），在emg2pose与SynapMyo上三种泛化设定下关节角精度均超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG可实现连续手势估计，但学习跨用户、跨动作可泛化的表征仍具挑战性；同时缺乏大规模、多用户的高质量EMG-姿态数据支撑该方向研究。

Method: InterBand-EMG：将频带特定量化与跨频带重建结合，并引入结构化掩码码本预测，以自监督方式学习可迁移的EMG表征用于手势追踪。数据侧构建SynapMyo：八通道EMG到姿态数据集，1,227.74手时、499名用户、68类动作。

Result: 在emg2pose与SynapMyo两数据集、三种泛化设定下，关节角精度均优于现有SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 频带结构化与跨频带信息融合的表征学习有效提升sEMG手势追踪的泛化能力；SynapMyo为大规模多用户EMG-姿态研究提供基准，代码与数据将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) enables continuous hand-pose estimation, but learning representations that generalize across users and movements remains challenging. We introduce InterBand-EMG, which combines band-specific quantization with cross-band reconstruction and structured masked code prediction to learn transferable EMG representations for hand-pose tracking. We also introduce SynapMyo, an eight-channel EMG-to-pose dataset comprising 1,227.74 hand-hours from 499 users across 68 movement categories. Experiments on emg2pose and SynapMyo show that InterBand-EMG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in joint-angle accuracy across all three generalization settings on both datasets. The source code and the SynapMyo dataset will be publicly released upon acceptance of this paper.

</details>


### [68] [EgoLAM: Learning Latent Actions from Hand and Head Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27290)
*Ziyi Ding,Yiwei Wang,Zhongxing Wei,Xianxin Lai,Yechi Liu,Xiaodi Huang,Jinghua Hou,Xiaoquan Sun,Haijier Chen,Zhe Liu,Yinan Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoLAM 通过以实测腕部运动监督动作编码、并将相机运动单独送入前向模型，从自我中心视频中提取仅反映操作的隐动作码，在两帧 RGB 输入下取得最高手部运动解码分数，并在 LIBERO 三套任务上以 79.38% 平均成功率显著超越专家行为克隆与最强对比隐动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐动作模型通过把相邻两帧的变化作为训练目标，使机器人策略能从视频中学习。但在自我中心视频中，图像变化来源混杂：杯子在画面中移动既可能是手推动，也可能是佩戴者转身。两者都有助于预测下一帧，但只有前者刻画了操作行为。因此需要将动作码与相机运动解耦，使隐动作码只描述手对物体的操作。

Method: EgoLAM 在训练时用实测腕部运动监督动作码，同时把相机运动作为独立输入提供给前向模型，从而即使佩戴者在推动杯子时转身，训练目标仍描述手的推动方式。动作编码器在每只手周围池化物体槽（object slots），腕部平移与旋转可在统一参考系下从该动作码解码。提取动作码只需两帧 RGB 图像。

Result: 除 LARYBench 的分类与回归指标外，作者还测量冻结动作码保留多少腕部运动信息，以及对合成视角或光照变化的响应强度。在八个评估模型中，EgoLAM 的手部运动解码分数最高。在 LIBERO 三套任务上，使用其动作码预训练、再用专家示教微调的策略平均成功率达 79.38%，比专家行为克隆高 28.73 个百分点，平均比每套任务中最强的其他隐动作模型高 9.76 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过分离腕部运动与相机运动进行监督，可得到紧凑、可解耦、且仅需两帧即可提取的隐动作表示；该表示在运动信息保留性与视觉扰动鲁棒性上表现优异，并能作为预训练目标显著提升下游模仿学习策略的成功率。

Abstract: Latent action models let a robot policy learn from video by turning the change between two frames into a training target. In egocentric video, a cup can move across the image because a hand pushes it or because the wearer turns. Both changes help predict the next frame, but only the first describes manipulation. During training, EgoLAM supervises the action code with measured wrist motion and supplies camera motion separately to the forward model. Even when the wearer turns while pushing a cup, the training target still describes how the hand pushed it. The action encoder pools object slots around each hand, and wrist translation and rotation can be decoded from the code in a common reference frame. Extracting a code then requires only two RGB frames. Alongside LARYBench classification and regression, we measure how much wrist motion a frozen code retains and how strongly it responds to a synthetic view or lighting change. EgoLAM has the highest hand-motion decoding score among eight evaluated models. Across three LIBERO suites, policies pretrained on its codes and fine-tuned on expert demonstrations achieve 79.38% mean success, 28.73 points above expert behavior cloning and 9.76 points above the strongest other latent action model in each suite on average.

</details>


### [69] [A Vision-Based Framework for Manual Assembly Verification and Adaptive Operator Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27293)
*Naveen Joseph Delphin,Leonardo Venturoso,Isabella Soraruf,Marco Todescato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于单目俯视RGB相机的模块化装配过程监控框架（目标检测、手部运动跟踪、区域感知交互检测、有限状态验证、姿态分类、可配置视觉引导），在8名被试的气动活塞装拆实验中比较三种引导条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工装配难以监控，因为操作者动作、零部件与任务进度需联合解读；现有方案常依赖多传感器或复杂设备。

Method: 单相机模块化流水线：目标检测+手部运动跟踪+区域感知交互检测+有限状态机流程验证+任务相关姿态分类+可配置视觉引导；气动活塞装配/拆卸实验，8名被试，full/mid/no guidance三条件，混合效应模型分析。

Result: 混合效应分析显示引导条件与运动质量复合指标相关，mid与no guidance的估计得分低于full guidance；但条件×试次交互不显著，学习轨迹差异仅为描述性；完成时间与失败阶段数无显著条件差异。

Conclusion: 验证了视觉感知、流程验证与可配置反馈在单相机工作流中的集成可行性；依据操作者熟练度自适应选择引导强度仍是未来研究方向。

Abstract: Manual assembly remains difficult to monitor because operator actions, components, and task progress must be interpreted together. This paper presents a modular framework based on a single overhead red-green-blue (RGB) camera, combining object detection, hand-motion tracking, region-aware interaction detection, finite-state verification, task-specific pose classification, and configurable visual guidance. The system was evaluated during pneumatic-piston assembly and disassembly with eight participants under three assistance conditions: full, mid, and no guidance. Mixed-effects analysis identified an association between assistance condition and a composite measure of motion quality, with lower estimated scores under mid and no guidance relative to full guidance. However, the condition-by-trial interaction was not statistically significant, and the estimated differences in learning trajectories remain descriptive. No condition differences were detected in completion time or failed-stage counts. The study demonstrates the integration of visual perception, procedural verification, and configurable operator feedback in a single-camera workflow. Selecting assistance according to operator expertise remains a design objective for future investigation.

</details>


### [70] [Can Reasoning and Image Description Enhance Yes/No Visual Mathematical Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27302)
*Guangcheng Song,Weichuan Wang,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过配对评测与CoT提示、文本描述替换/增强视觉输入等条件，考察开源VLM的视觉数学判断能力，发现CoT推理有效而文本描述收益不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（VLM）在通用视觉任务上表现优异，但对数学图像的理解能力仍然有限；本文探究显式推理与文本图像描述能否提升VLM在二元是/否视觉数学问题上的表现。

Method: 构建配对评测集，在多种提示与输入条件下基准测试主流开源VLM；采用Chain-of-Thought（CoT）提示，并以文本描述替换或增强视觉输入，从而隔离并评估数学理解能力。

Result: 显式推理可提升性能，尤其在配对一致性准确率上提升明显；文本图像描述并不能稳定带来增益，甚至可能造成性能下降。

Conclusion: 推理对于稳定可靠的视觉数学判断至关重要，而基于描述的中间表示需要更审慎的设计。
